// ride to the far call and reverse there\n        }\n\n        _nearestAnyStop(fromFloor, excludeFloor) {\n            let best = null, bestDist = Infinity;\n            for (let f = 0; f < this.floorCount; f++) {\n                if (f === excludeFloor) continue;\n                if (this.destinations.has(f) || this.upCalls.has(f) || this.downCalls.has(f)) {\n                    const d = Math.abs(f - fromFloor);\n                    if (d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; best = f; }\n                }\n            }\n            return best;\n        }\n\n        _pickNextTarget() {\n            // Called at door-close (and from IDLE). Decide the next target floor.\n            const here = this.currentFloor;\n            const hasRiders = this.passengers.size > 0 || this.pendingBoarders.size > 0;\n\n            // Passenger destinations outrank everything else, especially\n            // same-floor hall calls (no full-car lobby starvation).\n            if (hasRiders && this.destinations.size > 0) {\n                let target = null;\n                if (this.direction !== 0) {\n                    target = this._nearestDestInDirection(this.direction, here);\n                    if (target === null) target = this._nearestDestInDirection(-this.direction, here);\n                }\n                if (target === null) {\n                    let bestDist = Infinity;\n                    this.destinations.forEach((f) => {\n                        const d = Math.abs(f - here);\n                        if (f !== here && d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; target = f; }\n                    });\n                }\n                if (target !== null) return target;\n            }\n\n            // Continue in current direction if there's work ahead.\n            if (this.direction !== 0) {\n                const ahead = this._nearestStopInDirection(this.direction, here);\n                if (ahead !== null) return ahead;\n                const behind = this._nearestStopInDirection(-this.direction, here);\n                if (behind !== null) return behind;\n            }\n\n            // Same-floor hall call while idle/empty. Skipped right after a\n            // door cycle at this floor (servedThisDoorCycle) so leftover\n            // re-pressed calls can't force an immediate reopen; once the car\n            // is IDLE that flag clears and a fresh call reopens normally.\n            if (!hasRiders && !this.servedThisDoorCycle &&\n                (this.upCalls.has(here) || this.downCalls.has(here))) {\n                return here;\n            }\n\n            // Nearest active call/destination anywhere else.\n            const exclude = this.servedThisDoorCycle ? here : -1;\n            const any = this._nearestAnyStop(here, exclude);\n            if (any !== null) return any;\n            return null;\n        }\n\n        _nearestDestInDirection(dir, fromFloor) {\n            if (dir > 0) {\n                for (let f = fromFloor + 1; f < this.floorCount; f++) {\n                    if (this.destinations.has(f)) return f;\n                }\n            } else if (dir < 0) {\n                for (let f = fromFloor - 1; f >= 0; f--) {\n                    if (this.destinations.has(f)) return f;\n                }\n            }\n            return null;\n        }\n\n        _arriveServe() {\n            // Door-open arrival housekeeping: clear served stops.\n            const here = this.currentFloor;\n            this.destinations.delete(here);\n            if (this.direction > 0) {\n                this.upCalls.delete(here);\n                if (!this._hasWorkInDirection(1, here)) {\n                    // no more work ahead — also serve the down call here, and reverse intent\n                    this.downCalls.delete(here);\n                    if (this._hasWorkInDirection(-1, here)) this.direction = -1;\n                    else this.direction = 0;\n                }\n            } else if (this.direction < 0) {\n                this.downCalls.delete(here);\n                if (!this._hasWorkInDirection(-1, here)) {\n                    this.upCalls.delete(here);\n                    if (this._hasWorkInDirection(1, here)) this.direction = 1;\n                    else this.direction = 0;\n                }\n            } else {\n                // idle arrival: infer direction from remaining work, clear both\n                if (this.upCalls.has(here) && this.downCalls.has(here)) {\n                    const up = this._hasWorkInDirection(1, here);\n                    const down = this._hasWorkInDirection(-1, here);\n                    this.upCalls.delete(here);\n                    this.downCalls.delete(here);\n                    this.direction = up ? 1 : (down ? -1 : 0);\n                } else if (this.upCalls.has(here)) {\n                    this.upCalls.delete(here);\n                    this.direction = 1;\n                } else if (this.downCalls.has(here)) {\n                    this.downCalls.delete(here);\n                    this.direction = -1;\n                }\n            }\n            this.servedThisDoorCycle = true;\n            this.lastServedFloor = here;\n        }\n\n        // ---- main tick --------------------------------------------------\n\n        tick(dt) {\n            if (dt <= 0) return;\n            // Cap a single physics step; long frames advance in sub-steps so\n            // a high timeScale doesn't tunnel the car through floors.\n            let remaining = dt;\n            const MAX_STEP = 0.25;\n            while (remaining > 0) {\n                const step = Math.min(MAX_STEP, remaining);\n                const wasOpen = this.state === STATES.DOOR_OPEN;\n                this._step(step);\n                remaining -= step;\n                // Yield the moment the doors reach DOOR_OPEN: at high time\n                // scales a whole frame can exceed the min-open window, and\n                // riders/waiters need one observable DOOR_OPEN frame to\n                // register boarding/disembarking before the timer runs.\n                if (!wasOpen && this.state === STATES.DOOR_OPEN) break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        _step(dt) {\n            switch (this.state) {\n                case a.hi + (b.hi - a.hi) * t;\n    }\n\n    // ---------------- seat reservations ----------------\n\n    const seatReservations = new Set();   // \"floor:wpName\"\n\n    function seatKey(floor, wp) { return floor + \":\" + wp; }\n\n    function reserveSeat(agent, floor, wp) {\n        const key = seatKey(floor, wp);\n        if (seatReservations.has(key)) return false;\n        if (agent.reservedSeat) seatReservations.delete(agent.reservedSeat);\n        seatReservations.add(key);\n        agent.reservedSeat = key;\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    function releaseSeat(agent) {\n        if (agent.reservedSeat) {\n            seatReservations.delete(agent.reservedSeat);\n            agent.reservedSeat = null;\n        }\n    }\n\n    function reserveFrom(agent, floor, wpList) {\n        const order = wpList.slice();\n        for (let i = order.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {\n            const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));\n            const tmp = order[i]; order[i] = order[j]; order[j] = tmp;\n        }\n        for (let i = 0; i < order.length; i++) {\n            if (reserveSeat(agent, floor, order[i])) return order[i];\n        }\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    function reserveConfSeat(agent, floor) {\n        return reserveFrom(agent, floor, world.floors[floor].confSeats);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------- agents ----------------\n\n    const agents = [];\n    let targetOccupancy = DEFAULT_OCCUPANCY;\n\n    function sampleWorkerSchedule(a) {\n        a.arrivalTime = rand(495, 570);            // 8:15..9:30\n        a.lunchTime = rand(690, 810);              // 11:30..13:30\n        a.lunchDuration = rand(25, 60);\n        a.departureTime = Math.random() < 0.15 ? rand(1110, 1185) : rand(1005, 1110);\n        a.hasLunched = false;\n        a.plannedMeetingTimes = [];\n        const n = randInt(0, 2);\n        if (n >= 1) a.plannedMeetingTimes.push(rand(575, 690));   // morning\n        if (n >= 2) a.plannedMeetingTimes.push(rand(815, 985));   // afternoon\n    }\n\n    function sampleVisitorSchedule(a) {\n        a.arrivalTime = rand(510, 1020);           // 8:30..17:00\n        a.hasLunched = true;\n        a.plannedMeetingTimes = [];\n        a.departureTime = 24 * 60 + 999;           // visitors leave via their plan\n    }\n\n    function buildAgents() {\n        const allDesks = [];\n        for (let f = 1; f < WORLD.FLOOR_COUNT; f++) {\n            world.floors[f].desks.forEach(function (d) { allDesks.push(d); });\n        }\n        for (let id = 0; id < MAX_OCCUPANCY; id++) {\n            const role = id < MAX_WORKERS ? \"WORKER\" : \"VISITOR\";\n            const a = {\n                id: id, role: role,\n                name: NAMES[id % NAMES.length] + (id >= NAMES.length ? \"-\" + id : \"\"),\n                group: createPerson({}),\n                state: id < targetOccupancy ? \"AWAY\" : \"DISABLED\",\n                plan: [], currentAction: null,\n                floorIdx: 0, reservedSeat: null,\n                homeFloor: null, deskId: null, deskWp: null, doorWp: null\n            };\n            if (role === \"WORKER\") {\n                const d = allDesks[id];\n                a.homeFloor = d.floor; a.deskId = d.id;\n                a.deskWp = d.deskWp; a.doorWp = d.doorWp;\n                sampleWorkerSchedule(a);\n            } else {\n                sampleVisitorSchedule(a);\n            }\n            agents.push(a);\n        }\n    }\n\n    function countPresent() {\n        let n = 0;\n        for (let i = 0; i < agents.length; i++) {\n            const s = agents[i].state;\n            if (s !== \"AWAY\" && s !== \"GONE\" && s !== \"DISABLED\") n++;\n        }\n        return n;\n    }\n\n    function resetDay() {\n        agents.forEach(function (a) {\n            if (a.group.parent) a.group.parent.remove(a.group);\n            releaseSeat(a);\n            a.plan = []; a.currentAction = null;\n            a.group.userData.isSitting = false;\n            a.group.userData.isWalking = false;\n            a.floorIdx = 0;\n            a.state = a.id < targetOccupancy ? \"AWAY\" : \"DISABLED\";\n            if (a.role === \"WORKER\") sampleWorkerSchedule(a);\n            else sampleVisitorSchedule(a);\n        });\n        seatReservations.clear();\n        elevator.reset();   // phantom passengers would jam the next day\n    }\n\n    function applyOccupancy() {\n        agents.forEach(function (a) {\n            if (a.id < targetOccupancy) {\n                if (a.state === \"DISABLED\") {\n                    a.state = \"AWAY\";\n                    if (Clock.simMinute > a.arrivalTime && Clock.simMinute < 960) {\n                        a.arrivalTime = Clock.simMinute + rand(1, 15);\n                    }\n                }\n            } else if (a.state === \"AWAY\") {\n                a.state = \"DISABLED\";\n                // mid-day agents finish naturally; parked on day-wrap\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    function topUpVisitors() {\n        const m = Clock.simMinute;\n        if (m < 480 || m > 1050) return;   // business hours only\n        let incoming = 0;\n        agents.forEach(function (a) {\n            if (a.state === \"AWAY\" && a.arrivalTime <= m + 6) incoming++;\n        });\n        let deficit = targetOccupancy - countPresent() - incoming;\n        if (deficit <= 0) return;\n        for (let i = 0; i < agents.length && deficit > 0; i++) {\n            const a = agents[i];\n            if (a.role !== \"VISITOR\" || a.id >= targetOccupancy) continue;\n            if (a.state === \"GONE\" || (a.state === \"AWAY\" && a.arrivalTime > m + 6)) {\n                a.arrivalTime = m + rand(0, 6);\n                a.state = \"AWAY\";\n                deficit--;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ---------------- primitive actions ----------------\n\n    function A(type, props) {\n        const a = { type: type };\n        if (props) for (const k in props) a[k] = props[k];\n        return a;\n    }\n\n    function dirOf(from, to) { return to > from ? 1 : -1; }\n\n    // personal waiting spot in front of the elevator: spread agents out\n    function panelWaitSpot(agent, floor) {\n        const ang = agent.id * 2.39996;\n        const r = 0.55 + (agent.id % 4) * 0.3;\n        let x = Math.cos(ang) * r * 1.7;\n        let z = 2.45 + Math.abs(Math.sin(ang)) * r * 0.9 + 0.15;\n        x = Math.max(-2.3, Math.min(2.3, x));\n        z = Math.max(1.95, Math.min(4.4, z));\n        return new THREE.Vector3(x, floor * FH, z);\n    }\n\n    function moveToward(agent, target, dt, speed) {\n        // returns remaining distance after the move; rotates to face motion\n        const g = agent.group;\n        const dx = target.x - g.position.x;\n        const dz = target.z - g.position.z;\n        const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);\n        if (dist < 1e-6) return 0;\n        const step = Math.min(dist, (speed || WALK_SPEED) * dt);\n        g.position.x += dx / dist * step;\n        g.position.z += dz / dist * step;\n        g.rotation.y = Math.atan2(dx, dz);\n        return dist - step;\n    }\n\n    function startAction(agent, act) {\n        const g = agent.group;\n        switch (act.type) {\n            case \"WALK_TO_WP\": {\n                const nodes = world.floors[act.floor].nodes;\n                const fromName = world.nearestNodeName(nodes, g.position);\n                const path = bfsPath(nodes, fromName, act.wp) || [nodes[act.wp].pos.clone()];\n                act._path = path;\n                act._i = 0;\n                act._stallT = 0; act._prev = g.position.clone();\n                g.userData.isWalking = true;\n                g.userData.isSitting = false;\n                break;\n            }\n            case \"WAIT_AT_PANEL\":\n                act._spot = panelWaitSpot(agent, act.floor);\n                g.userData.isWalking = false;\n                break;\n            case \"ENTER_ELEVATOR\":\n                act._phase = 0;\n                act._stallT = 0;\n                g.userData.isWalking = true;\n                break;\n            case \"EXIT_ELEVATOR\": {\n                elevator.registerDisembark(agent);\n                // reparent car -> scene, preserving world position\n                const wp = new THREE.Vector3();\n                g.getWorldPosition(wp);\n                scene.add(g);\n                g.position.set(wp.x, act.floor * FH, wp.z);\n                agent.floorIdx = act.floor;\n                const spot = panelWaitSpot(agent, act.floor);\n                act._target = spot;\n                act._stallT = 0;\n                g.userData.isWalking = true;\n                break;\n            }\n            case \"WAIT_SIM\":\n                act._untilMin = Clock.simMinute + act.minutes;  // resolved HERE, not at compile\n                g.userData.isWalking = false;\n                break;\n            default:\n                break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // returns true when the action is complete\n    function updateAction(agent, act, dt) {\n        const g = agent.group;\n        switch (act.type) {\n\n            case \"WALK_TO_WP\": {\n                const path = act._path;\n                if (act._i >= path.length) { g.userData.isWalking = false; return true; }\n                const target = path[act._i];\n                const before = g.position.distanceTo(target);\n                moveToward(agent, target, dt);\n                const moved = g.position.distanceTo(act._prev);\n                act._prev.copy(g.position);\n                if (moved < 0.005) {\n                    act._stallT += dt;\n                    if (act._stallT > 1.2) { act._i++; act._stallT = 0; }  // skip blocked waypoint\n                } else {\n                    act._stallT = 0;\n                }\n                if (before < 0.1 || g.position.distanceTo(target) < 0.1) act._i++;\n                if (act._i >= path.length) { g.userData.isWalking = false; return true; }\n                return false;\n            }\n\n            case \"WAIT_AT_PANEL\": {\n                // re-press the call every frame in case a cycle cleared it\n                if (act.dir > 0) elevator.callUp(act.floor); else elevator.callDown(act.floor);\n                // drift to the personal waiting spot so waiters don't stack\n                const d = g.position.distanceTo(act._spot);\n                if (d > 0.12) {\n                    g.userData.isWalking = true;\n                    moveToward(agent, act._spot, dt, WALK_SPEED * 0.7);\n                } else {\n                    g.userData.isWalking = false;\n                    g.rotation.y = Math.PI;   // face the elevator doors\n                }\n                return elevator.isAcceptingAt(act.floor, act.dir) &&\n                    elevator.currentCapacityFree() > 0;\n            }\n\n            case \"ENTER_ELEVATOR\": {\n                // Phases fall through within one call: at high time scales a\n                // single frame must be able to cover reserve -> walk ->\n                // reparent -> walk-in -> completeBoard, or the door timer\n                // (which also runs frames) abandons the boarder mid-walk.\n                const floor = agent.floorIdx;\n\n                if (act._phase === 0) {            // reserve a spot\n                    if (!(elevator.state === \"DOOR_OPEN\" && elevator.currentFloor === floor)) {\n                        requeueBoard(agent, act); return true;\n                    }\n                    const spot = elevator.reserveBoardingSpot(agent);\n                    if (!spot) { requeueBoard(agent, act); return true; }\n                    act._spot = spot;\n                    const laneX = Math.max(-0.75, Math.min(0.75, spot.local.x));\n                    act._threshold = new THREE.Vector3(laneX, floor * FH, 1.45);\n                    act._phase = 1;\n                    act._prev = g.position.clone();\n                    g.userData.isWalking = true;\n                }\n\n                if (act._phase === 1) {            // walk to the door threshold (world)\n                    if (!(elevator.state === \"DOOR_OPEN\" && elevator.currentFloor === floor)) {\n                        elevator.cancelBoard(agent);   // car slipped away — start over\n                        requeueBoard(agent, act);\n                        return true;\n                    }\n                    moveToward(agent, act._threshold, dt);\n                    const moved = g.position.distanceTo(act._prev);\n                    act._prev.copy(g.position);\n                    if (moved < 0.005) {\n                        act._stallT += dt;\n                        if (act._stallT > 1.5) {   // crowd-block recovery: force through\n                            g.position.copy(act._threshold);\n                            act._stallT = 0;\n                        }\n                    } else act._stallT = 0;\n                    if (g.position.distanceTo(act._threshold) < 0.1) {\n                        // reparent scene -> car\n                        const car = elevator.car;\n                        g.position.set(g.position.x - car.position.x, 0, g.position.z - car.position.z);\n                        car.add(g);\n                        act._phase = 2;            // fall through this frame\n                    } else return false;\n                }\n\n                // phase 2: walk to the reserved interior spot (car-local)\n                if (!elevator.pendingBoarders.has(agent) && !elevator.passengers.has(agent)) {\n                    // force-abandoned by the door safety cap — step back out\n                    const wp = new THREE.Vector3();\n                    g.getWorldPosition(wp);\n                    scene.add(g);\n                    g.position.set(wp.x, agent.floorIdx * FH, Math.max(wp.z, 2.0));\n                    requeueBoard(agent, act);\n                    return true;\n                }\n                moveToward(agent, act._spot.local, dt);\n                act._stallT += dt;\n                if (g.position.distanceTo(act._spot.local) < 0.08 || act._stallT > 2.5) {\n                    g.position.set(act._spot.local.x, 0, act._spot.local.z);\n                    elevator.completeBoard(agent);\n                    g.rotation.y = 0;          // face the doors\n                    g.userData.isWalking = false;\n                    return true;\n                }\n                return false;\n            }\n\n            case \"PRESS_FLOOR\":\n                // exact destination from the plan compiler — never floor+dir\n                elevator.pressDestination(act.floor);\n                return true;\n\n            case \"WAIT_FOR_FLOOR\":\n                if (elevator.state === \"DOOR_OPEN\" && elevator.currentFloor === act.floor) return true;\n                // safety: re-press if our destination got cleared somehow\n                if (!elevator.destinations.has(act.floor) && elevator.currentFloor !== act.floor) {\n                    elevator.pressDestination(act.floor);\n                }\n                return false;\n\n            case \"EXIT_ELEVATOR\": {\n                const left = moveToward(agent, act._target, dt);\n                act._stallT += dt;\n                if (left < 0.12 || act._stallT > 2.5) {\n                    elevator.completeDisembark(agent);\n                    g.userData.isWalking = false;\n                    return true;\n                }\n                return false;\n            }\n\n            case \"SIT\": {\n                const fl = world.floors[act.floor];\n                const t = fl.sitTargets[act.wp];\n                const pos = fl.nodes[act.wp].pos;\n                if (t && t.sit) {\n                    g.position.set(pos.x, act.floor * FH - SIT_DROP, pos.z);\n                    g.rotation.y = t.facing;\n                    g.userData.isSitting = true;\n                } else {\n                    // standing waypoint: jitter a small ring so two agents\n                    // assigned the same spot don't overlap exactly\n                    const ang = rand(0, Math.PI * 2);\n                    const r = rand(0.35, 0.75);\n                    g.position.set(pos.x + Math.cos(ang) * r, act.floor * FH, pos.z + Math.sin(ang) * r);\n                    g.rotation.y = t ? t.facing : rand(0, Math.PI * 2);\n                    g.userData.isSitting = false;\n                }\n                g.userData.isWalking = false;\n                return true;\n            }\n\n            case \"STAND\":\n                g.userData.isSitting = false;\n                g.position.y = (g.parent === elevator.car) ? 0 : agent.floorIdx * FH;\n                // NOTE: seat reservation is NOT released here — see RELEASE_SEAT\n                return true;\n\n            case \"RELEASE_SEAT\":\n                releaseSeat(agent);\n                return true;\n\n            case \"WAIT_SIM\":\n                return Clock.simMinute >= act._untilMin;\n\n            case \"EXIT_BUILDING\":\n                if (g.parent) g.parent.remove(g);\n                agent.state = \"GONE\";\n                g.userData.isWalking = false;\n                return true;\n\n            case \"ENTER_STATE\":\n                agent.state = act.state;\n                return true;\n\n            case \"MARK_LUNCHED\":\n                agent.hasLunched = true;\n                return true;\n\n            case \"PICK_NEXT_ACTIVITY\":\n                chooseNextActivity(agent);\n                return true;\n\n            default:\n                return true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    function requeueBoard(agent, act) {\n        // car left or filled before we could board: wait + try again\n        const floor = agent.floorIdx;\n        if (act.dir > 0) elevator.callUp(floor); else elevator.callDown(floor);\n        agent.plan.unshift(\n            A(\"WAIT_AT_PANEL\", { floor: floor, dir: act.dir, toFloor: act.toFloor }),\n            A(\"ENTER_ELEVATOR\", { toFloor: act.toFloor, dir: act.dir })\n        );\n    }\n\n    // ---------------- plan compilers ----------------\n\n    function ride(from, to) {\n        if (from === to) return [];\n        const dir = dirOf(from, to);\n        return [\n            A(\"WALK_TO_WP\", { floor: from, wp: \"elevWait\" }),\n            A(\"ENTER_STATE\", { state: \"WAITING_ELEVATOR\" }),\n            A(\"WAIT_AT_PANEL\", { floor: from, dir: dir, toFloor: to }),\n            A(\"ENTER_ELEVATOR\", { toFloor: to, dir: dir }),\n            A(\"ENTER_STATE\", { state: \"IN_CAR\" }),\n            A(\"PRESS_FLOOR\", { floor: to }),       // explicit toFloor, not floor+dir\n            A(\"WAIT_FOR_FLOOR\", { floor: to }),\n            A(\"EXIT_ELEVATOR\", { floor: to }),\n            A(\"ENTER_STATE\", { state: \"ON_FLOOR\" })\n        ];\n    }\n\n    function backToDesk(a) {\n        return [\n            A(\"WALK_TO_WP\", { floor: a.homeFloor, wp: a.doorWp }),\n            A(\"WALK_TO_WP\", { floor: a.homeFloor, wp: a.deskWp }),\n            A(\"SIT\", { floor: a.homeFloor, wp: a.deskWp }),\n            A(\"ENTER_STATE\", { state: \"AT_DESK\" }),\n            A(\"WAIT_SIM\", { minutes: rand(18, 65) }),\n            A(\"PICK_NEXT_ACTIVITY\")\n        ];\n    }\n\n    function planArriveToDesk(a) {\n        return [\n            A(\"ENTER_STATE\", { state: \"ARRIVING\" }),\n            A(\"WALK_TO_WP\", { floor: 0, wp: \"entrance\" })\n        ].concat(ride(0, a.homeFloor)).concat(backToDesk(a));\n    }\n\n    function planGoToLunch(a) {\n        const lobby = world.floors[0];\n        let seat = reserveFrom(a, 0, lobby.bistroChairs);\n        if (!seat) seat = reserveFrom(a, 0, lobby.frontLoungeSeats.concat(lobby.backLoungeSeats));\n        const lunchWp = seat || pick(lobby.standSpots);\n        const seatActs = [A(\"WALK_TO_WP\", { floor: 0, wp: lunchWp }),\n        A(\"SIT\", { floor: 0, wp: lunchWp })];\n        return [\n            A(\"STAND\"), A(\"ENTER_STATE\", { state: \"ON_FLOOR\" }),\n            A(\"WALK_TO_WP\", { floor: a.homeFloor, wp: a.doorWp })\n        ].concat(ride(a.homeFloor, 0))\n            .concat(seatActs)\n            .concat([\n                A(\"ENTER_STATE\", { state: \"AT_LUNCH\" }),\n                A(\"WAIT_SIM\", { minutes: a.lunchDuration }),\n                A(\"MARK_LUNCHED\"),\n                A(\"STAND\"), A(\"RELEASE_SEAT\")\n            ])\n            .concat(ride(0, a.homeFloor))\n            .concat(backToDesk(a));\n    }\n\n    function planVisitLounge(a) {\n        const fl = world.floors[a.homeFloor];\n        let seat = reserveFrom(a, a.homeFloor, fl.loungeSpots);\n        if (!seat) seat = pick(fl.standSpots);   // stand at the cooler instead\n        return [\n            A(\"STAND\"), A(\"ENTER_STATE\", { state: \"ON_FLOOR\" }),\n            A(\"WALK_TO_WP\", { floor: a.homeFloor, wp: a.doorWp }),\n            A(\"WALK_TO_WP\", { floor: a.homeFloor, wp: seat }),\n            A(\"SIT\", { floor: a.homeFloor, wp: seat }),\n            A(\"ENTER_STATE\", { state: \"AT_BREAK\" }),\n            A(\"WAIT_SIM\", { minutes: rand(5, 12) }),\n            A(\"STAND\"), A(\"RELEASE_SEAT\")\n        ].concat(backToDesk(a));\n    }\n\n    function planAttendMeeting(a) {\n        const mf = Math.random() < 0.65 ? a.homeFloor : randInt(1, WORLD.FLOOR_COUNT - 1);\n        const seat = reserveConfSeat(a, mf);\n        if (!seat) return planVisitLounge(a);    // all four seats taken\n        return [\n            A(\"STAND\"), A(\"ENTER_STATE\", { state: \"ON_FLOOR\" }),\n            A(\"WALK_TO_WP\", { floor: a.homeFloor, wp: a.doorWp })\n        ].concat(ride(a.homeFloor, mf))\n            .concat([\n                A(\"WALK_TO_WP\", { floor: mf, wp: seat }),\n                A(\"SIT\", { floor: mf, wp: seat }),\n                A(\"ENTER_STATE\", { state: \"IN_MEETING\" }),\n                A(\"WAIT_SIM\", { minutes: rand(22, 45) }),\n                A(\"STAND\"), A(\"RELEASE_SEAT\")\n            ])\n            .concat(ride(mf, a.homeFloor))\n            .concat(backToDesk(a));\n    }\n\n    function planVisitCoworker(a) {\n        const candidates = agents.filter(function (o) {\n            return o !== a && o.role === \"WORKER\" && o.state === \"AT_DESK\";\n        });\n        if (!candidates.length) {\n            return [A(\"WAIT_SIM\", { minutes: rand(10, 25) }), A(\"PICK_NEXT_ACTIVITY\")];\n        }\n        const c = pick(candidates);\n        return [\n            A(\"STAND\"), A(\"ENTER_STATE\", { state: \"ON_FLOOR\" }),\n            A(\"WALK_TO_WP\", { floor: a.homeFloor, wp: a.doorWp })\n        ].concat(ride(a.homeFloor, c.homeFloor))\n            .concat([\n                A(\"WALK_TO_WP\", { floor: c.homeFloor, wp: c.doorWp }),\n                A(\"ENTER_STATE\", { state: \"VISITING\" }),\n                A(\"WAIT_SIM\", { minutes: rand(6, 18) })\n            ])\n            .concat(ride(c.homeFloor, a.homeFloor))\n            .concat(backToDesk(a));\n    }\n\n    function planLeaveBuilding(a) {\n        return [\n            A(\"STAND\"), A(\"RELEASE_SEAT\"),\n            A(\"ENTER_STATE\", { state: \"LEAVING\" })\n        ].concat(ride(a.floorIdx, 0)).concat([\n            A(\"WALK_TO_WP\", { floor: 0, wp: \"entrance\" }),\n            A(\"WALK_TO_WP\", { floor: 0, wp: \"outside\" }),\n            A(\"EXIT_BUILDING\")\n        ]);\n    }\n\n    function planVisitorVisit(a) {\n        const lobby = world.floors[0];\n        const r = Math.random();\n        let acts = [];\n\n        function sitFor(floor, wp, lo, hi, state) {\n            return [\n                A(\"WALK_TO_WP\", { floor: floor, wp: wp }),\n                A(\"SIT\", { floor: floor, wp: wp }),\n                A(\"ENTER_STATE\", { state: state || \"VISITING\" }),\n                A(\"WAIT_SIM\", { minutes: rand(lo, hi) }),\n                A(\"STAND\"), A(\"RELEASE_SEAT\")\n            ];\n        }\n        function loiter() {\n            return sitFor(0, pick(lobby.standSpots), 4, 15);\n        }\n\n        if (r < 0.10) {                                   // bistro table\n            const s = reserveFrom(a, 0, lobby.bistroChairs);\n            acts = s ? sitFor(0, s, 8, 25) : loiter();\n        } else if (r < 0.16) {                            // cafe counter\n            acts = sitFor(0, lobby.cafeOrder, 2, 6);\n        } else if (r < 0.30) {                            // front lounge\n            const s = reserveFrom(a, 0, lobby.frontLoungeSeats);\n            acts = s ? sitFor(0, s, 6, 20) : loiter();\n        } else if (r < 0.42) {                            // back lounge / pit\n            const s = reserveFrom(a, 0, lobby.backLoungeSeats.concat(lobby.pitSeats));\n            acts = s ? sitFor(0, s, 6, 20) : loiter();\n        } else if (r < 0.52) {                            // reception / kiosk / cooler\n            acts = sitFor(0, pick(lobby.quickStops), 2, 6);\n        } else if (r < 0.62) {                            // lobby loiter\n            acts = loiter();\n        } else if (r < 0.77) {                            // office-floor lounge\n            const f = randInt(1, WORLD.FLOOR_COUNT - 1);\n            const fl = world.floors[f];\n            let s = reserveFrom(a, f, fl.loungeSpots);\n            if (!s) s = pick(fl.standSpots);\n            acts = ride(0, f).concat(sitFor(f, s, 6, 18)).concat(ride(f, 0));\n        } else {                                          // sit in on a meeting\n            const f = randInt(1, WORLD.FLOOR_COUNT - 1);\n            const s = reserveConfSeat(a, f);\n            if (s) {\n                acts = ride(0, f)\n                    .concat(sitFor(f, s, 22, 45, \"IN_MEETING\"))\n                    .concat(ride(f, 0));\n            } else {\n                acts = loiter();                          // all four seats taken\n            }\n // fixed deterministic tick\nconst results = [];\n\nfunction test(name, fn) {\n    try {\n        fn();\n        results.push({ name, ok: true });\n        console.log(`PASS  ${name}`);\n    } catch (err) {\n        results.push({ name, ok: false, err });\n        console.log(`FAIL  ${name}`);\n        console.log(`      ${err.message}`);\n    }\n}\n\n// --- helpers ---------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction tickUntil(ev, pred, maxTicks, label) {\n    maxTicks = maxTicks || 5000;\n    for (let i = 0; i < maxTicks; i++) {\n        if (pred(ev)) return i;\n        ev.tick(DT);\n    }\n    throw new Error(`tickUntil(${label || \"predicate\"}) exceeded ${maxTicks} ticks ` +\n        `(state=${ev.state} floor=${ev.currentFloor} target=${ev.targetFloor})`);\n}\n\nfunction runUntilDoorOpenAt(ev, floor, maxTicks) {\n    return tickUntil(ev,\n        (e) => e.state === \"DOOR_OPEN\" && e.currentFloor === floor,\n        maxTicks, `DOOR_OPEN@${floor}`);\n}\n\nfunction runUntilDoorClosed(ev, maxTicks) {\n    // run until doors finish closing (state leaves DOOR_OPEN/DOOR_CLOSING)\n    tickUntil(ev, (e) => e.state === \"DOOR_OPEN\", maxTicks, \"reach DOOR_OPEN\");\n    return tickUntil(ev,\n        (e) => e.state === \"IDLE\" || e.state === \"MOVING\",\n        maxTicks, \"doors closed\");\n}\n\nfunction boardRider(ev, name, destFloor) {\n    const spot = ev.reserveBoardingSpot(name);\n    assert.ok(spot, `${name} should get a boarding spot`);\n    ev.completeBoard(name);\n    if (destFloor !== undefined && destFloor !== null) ev.pressDestination(destFloor);\n    return spot;\n}\n\n// --- 1. Lobby rush with more callers than capacity ---------------------------\n\ntest(\"lobby rush: full car leaves floor 0 despite re-pressed UP calls\", () => {\n    const ev = new ElevatorLogic({ floorCount: 6, maxCapacity: 4 });\n    ev.callUp(0);\n    runUntilDoorOpenAt(ev, 0);\n\n    // four riders board, press upper floors\n    boardRider(ev, \"p1\", 2);\n    boardRider(ev, \"p2\", 3);\n    boardRider(ev, \"p3\", 5);\n    boardRider(ev, \"p4\", 3);\n    assert.strictEqual(ev.currentCapacityFree(), 0, \"car should be full\");\n    assert.strictEqual(ev.reserveBoardingSpot(\"p5\"), null, \"5th rider must be rejected\");\n\n    // leftover lobby waiters keep pressing UP every tick\n    let sawReopenAtZero = false;\n    let leftFloorZero = false;\n    for (let i = 0; i < 600; i++) {\n        ev.callUp(0); // re-press like real waiters\n        const prevState = ev.state;\n        ev.tick(DT);\n        if (prevState === \"DOOR_CLOSING\" && ev.state === \"DOOR_OPENING\" && ev.currentFloor === 0) {\n            sawReopenAtZero = true;\n        }\n        if (ev.state === \"MOVING\" && ev.targetFloor > 0) { leftFloorZero = true; break; }\n    }\n    assert.ok(!sawReopenAtZero, \"doors must not reopen at floor 0 while destinations exist\");\n    assert.ok(leftFloorZero, \"car must depart upward with its passengers\");\n    assert.ok(ev.targetFloor > 0, `next target must be above floor 0, got ${ev.targetFloor}`);\n    assert.ok(ev.upCalls.has(0), \"leftover UP call stays queued for the next trip\");\n});\n\n// --- 2. Passenger destinations outrank same-floor hall calls -----------------\n\ntest(\"passenger destinations outrank same-floor hall calls\", () => {\n    const ev = new ElevatorLogic({ floorCount: 6, maxCapacity: 4 });\n    ev.callUp(0);\n    runUntilDoorOpenAt(ev, 0);\n    boardRider(ev, \"rider\", 4);\n\n    // a same-floor hall call appears right as the doors are closing\n    tickUntil(ev, (e) => e.state === \"DOOR_CLOSING\", 500, \"DOOR_CLOSING\");\n    ev.callUp(0);\n    let reopened = false;\n    for (let i = 0; i < 400; i++) {\n        const prev = ev.state;\n        ev.tick(DT);\n        if (prev === \"DOOR_CLOSING\" && ev.state === \"DOOR_OPENING\" && ev.currentFloor === 0) reopened = true;\n        if (ev.state === \"MOVING\") break;\n    }\n    assert.ok(!reopened, \"must not reopen at the same floor with a passenger aboard\");\n    assert.strictEqual(ev.targetFloor, 4, \"must head to the passenger's destination\");\n});\n\n// --- 3. Repeated hall-call pressing cannot starve riders ---------------------\n\ntest(\"repeated callUp(0) cannot starve riders' destinations\", () => {\n    const ev = new ElevatorLogic({ floorCount: 6, maxCapacity: 4 });\n    ev.callUp(0);\n    runUntilDoorOpenAt(ev, 0);\n    boardRider(ev, \"a\", 3);\n    boardRider(ev, \"b\", 5);\n\n    let reachedDest = false;\n    for (let i = 0; i < 3000; i++) {\n        ev.callUp(0); // hammer the lobby button every tick\n        ev.tick(DT);\n        if (ev.state === \"DOOR_OPEN\" && (ev.currentFloor === 3 || ev.currentFloor === 5)) {\n            reachedDest = true;\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    assert.ok(reachedDest, \"car must still reach a passenger destination\");\n});\n\n// --- 4. Opposite-direction calls wait their turn -----------------------------\n\ntest(\"down call below does not reverse an upward car with work above\", () => {\n    const ev = new ElevatorLogic({ floorCount: 6, maxCapacity: 4 });\n    ev.callUp(0);\n    runUntilDoorOpenAt(ev, 0);\n    boardRider(ev, \"up1\", 4);\n    boardRider(ev, \"up2\", 5);\n\n    runUntilDoorClosed(ev);\n    assert.strictEqual(ev.direction, 1, \"car should be heading up\");\n\n    // While moving up, a DOWN call arrives at floor 1 (below / behind)\n    ev.callDown(1);\n    ev.callDown(2);\n\n    // Car must serve 4 then 5 before turning around for the down calls.\n    runUntilDoorOpenAt(ev, 4);\n    assert.ok(ev.downCalls.has(1) && ev.downCalls.has(2), \"down calls still queued at floor 4\");\n    runUntilDoorOpenAt(ev, 5);\n    // after the top, it may reverse and serve the down calls\n    tickUntil(ev, (e) => e.state === \"DOOR_OPEN\" && (e.currentFloor === 2 || e.currentFloor === 1),\n        8000, \"serve down calls after reversing\");\n    assert.ok(true);\n});\n\n// --- 5. Door hold and safety cap ---------------------------------------------\n\ntest(\"doors hold for pending boarders, then MAX_DOOR_OPEN_S cap closes them\", () => {\n    const ev = new ElevatorLogic({ floorCount: 6, maxCapacity: 4 });\n    ev.callUp(0);\n    runUntilDoorOpenAt(ev, 0);\n\n    const spot = ev.reserveBoardingSpot(\"slowpoke\");\n    assert.ok(spot, \"slowpoke gets a spot\");\n    // never completes boarding…\n\n    // after MIN_DOOR_OPEN_S the doors must STILL be open (held by pendingBoarders)\n    const minTicks = Math.ceil((ev.MIN_DOOR_OPEN_S + 0.5) / DT);\n    for (let i = 0; i < minTicks; i++) ev.tick(DT);\n    assert.strictEqual(ev.state, \"DOOR_OPEN\", \"doors held open past min time by pending boarder\");\n\n    // …but MAX_DOOR_OPEN_S eventually force-closes\n    const capTicks = Math.ceil((ev.MAX_DOOR_OPEN_S + 1.0) / DT);\n    for (let i = 0; i < capTicks; i++) ev.tick(DT);\n    assert.notStrictEqual(ev.state, \"DOOR_OPEN\", \"safety cap must close the doors\");\n    assert.strictEqual(ev.pendingBoarders.size, 0, \"stuck boarder abandoned\");\n    assert.strictEqual(ev.spotOccupancy.filter(Boolean).length, 0, \"spot released\");\n\n    // also verify a disembarker holds the doors\n    const ev2 = new ElevatorLogic({ floorCount: 6, maxCapacity: 4 });\n    ev2.callUp(0);\n    runUntilDoorOpenAt(ev2, 0);\n    boardRider(ev2, \"r\", 3);\n    runUntilDoorOpenAt(ev2, 3);\n    ev2.registerDisembark(\"r\");\n    const holdTicks = Math.ceil((ev2.MIN_DOOR_OPEN_S + 1.0) / DT);\n    for (let i = 0; i < holdTicks; i++) ev2.tick(DT);\n    assert.strictEqual(ev2.state, \"DOOR_OPEN\", \"doors held for pending disembark\");\n    ev2.completeDisembark(\"r\");\n    tickUntil(ev2, (e) => e.state !== \"DOOR_OPEN\", 1000, \"close after disembark completes\");\n});\n\n// --- 6. s.lineTo(0.13, -0.1); s.lineTo(0, 0.13);\n        } else {\n            s.moveTo(-0.13, 0.1); s.lineTo(0.13, 0.1); s.lineTo(0, -0.13);\n        }\n        return new THREE.ShapeGeometry(s);\n    }\n\n    const ARROW_OFF_MAT = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x2a2f2a });\n    const ARROW_ON_MAT = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x39ff5e });\n\n    function makeCallPanel() {\n        const g = new THREE.Group();\n        const plate = box(0.55, 1.4, 0.05, solidMat(0x33363f));\n        plate.position.y = 0;\n        g.add(plate);\n\n        const upArrow = new THREE.Mesh(makeArrowGeometry(true), ARROW_OFF_MAT);\n        upArrow.position.set(0, 0.42, 0.032);\n        g.add(upArrow);\n\n        const downArrow = new THREE.Mesh(makeArrowGeometry(false), ARROW_OFF_MAT);\n        downArrow.position.set(0, 0.12, 0.032);\n        g.add(downArrow);\n\n        const tex = makeTextTexture(\"0\");\n        const disp = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.45, 0.45),\n            new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: tex }));\n        disp.position.set(0, -0.38, 0.032);\n        g.add(disp);\n\n        g.userData.setUp = function (on) { upArrow.material = on ? ARROW_ON_MAT : ARROW_OFF_MAT; };\n        g.userData.setDown = function (on) { downArrow.material = on ? ARROW_ON_MAT : ARROW_OFF_MAT; };\n        g.userData.setIndicator = function (text) { updateTextTexture(tex, text); };\n        return g;\n    }\n\n    function makeShaftIndicator() {\n        const tex = makeTextTexture(\"0\");\n        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.9, 0.9),\n            new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: tex }));\n        mesh.userData.setText = function (text) { updateTextTexture(tex, text); };\n        return mesh;\n    }\n\n    // ---------------- navigation graph ----------------\n\n    function addNode(nodes, name, x, y, z) {\n        nodes[name] = { pos: new THREE.Vector3(x, y, z), links: [] };\n    }\n\n    function link(nodes, a, b) {\n        if (!nodes[a] || !nodes[b]) {\n            console.warn(\"link: missing node\", a, b);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (nodes[a].links.indexOf(b) === -1) nodes[a].links.push(b);\n        if (nodes[b].links.indexOf(a) === -1) nodes[b].links.push(a);\n    }\n\n    function bfsPath(nodes, fromName, toName) {\n        if (!nodes[fromName] || !nodes[toName]) return null;\n        if (fromName === toName) return [nodes[toName].pos.clone()];\n        const prev = {}; prev[fromName] = fromName;\n        const queue = [fromName];\n        while (queue.length) {\n            const cur = queue.shift();\n            if (cur === toName) break;\n            const ls = nodes[cur].links;\n            for (let i = 0; i < ls.length; i++) {\n                const nb = ls[i];\n                if (!(nb in prev)) { prev[nb] = cur; queue.push(nb); }\n            }\n        }\n        if (!(toName in prev)) return null;\n        const names = [];\n        let cur = toName;\n        while (cur !== fromName) { names.push(cur); cur = prev[cur]; }\n        names.push(fromName);\n        names.reverse();\n        return names.map(function (n) { return nodes[n].pos.clone(); });\n    }\n\n    function nearestNodeName(nodes, pos) {\n        let best = null, bestD = Infinity;\n        for (const name in nodes) {\n            const d = nodes[name].pos.distanceToSquared(pos);\n            if (d < bestD) { bestD = d; best = name; }\n        }\n        return best;\n    }\n\n    // ---------------- wall building helpers ----------------\n\n    function wallX(group, x, z0, z1, y0, y1, mat) {\n        // wall in a constant-x plane\n        const h = y1 - y0, d = Math.abs(z1 - z0);\n        const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(d, h), mat);\n        m.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;\n        m.position.set(x, y0 + h / 2, (z0 + z1) / 2);\n        group.add(m);\n        return m;\n    }\n\n    function wallZ(group, z, x0, x1, y0, y1, mat) {\n        // wall in a constant-z plane\n        const h = y1 - y0, w = Math.abs(x1 - x0);\n        const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w, h), mat);\n        m.position.set((x0 + x1) / 2, y0 + h / 2, z);\n        group.add(m);\n        return m;\n    }\n\n    // ---------------- per-floor construction ----------------\n\n    const OFFICE_CENTERS = [-8.25, -2.75, 2.75, 8.25];\n    const OFFICE_LETTERS = [\"A\", \"B\", \"C\", \"D\"];\n    const DOOR_W = 1.2;\n\n    function addRingNodes(nodes, y) {\n        addNode(nodes, \"hallS\", 0, y, 3.3);\n        addNode(nodes, \"hallSE\", 2.6, y, 2.6);\n        addNode(nodes, \"hallE\", 2.6, y, 0);\n        addNode(nodes, \"hallNE\", 2.6, y, -2.6);\n        addNode(nodes, \"hallN\", 0, y, -2.45);\n        addNode(nodes, \"hallNW\", -2.6, y, -2.6);\n        addNode(nodes, \"hallW\", -2.6, y, 0);\n        addNode(nodes, \"hallSW\", -2.6, y, 2.6);\n        addNode(nodes, \"elevWait\", 0, y, 2.3);\n        const ring = [\"hallS\", \"hallSE\", \"hallE\", \"hallNE\", \"hallN\", \"hallNW\", \"hallW\", \"hallSW\"];\n        for (let i = 0; i < ring.length; i++) link(nodes, ring[i], ring[(i + 1) % ring.length]);\n        link(nodes, \"elevWait\", \"hallS\");\n    }\n\n    function placeChairAt(group, chair, x, y, z, ry) {\n        chair.position.set(x, y, z);\n        chair.rotation.y = ry;\n        group.add(chair);\n        return chair;\n    }\n\n    // Build one office floor (floors 1..5)\n    function buildOfficeFloor(group, floorNumber) {\n        const y = floorNumber * FH;\n        const nodes = {};\n        const sitTargets = {};\n        const desks = [];\n        addRingNodes(nodes, y);\n\n        // ---- interior walls ----\n        const wt = y, wtTop = y + FH;\n        // office front wall (z=-3) with 4 door gaps\n        const segs = [[-HW, OFFICE_CENTERS[0] - DOOR_W / 2]];\n        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n            segs.push([OFFICE_CENTERS[i] + DOOR_W / 2, OFFICE_CENTERS[i + 1] - DOOR_W / 2]);\n        }\n        segs.push([OFFICE_CENTERS[3] + DOOR_W / 2, HW]);\n        segs.forEach(function (s) { wallZ(group, -3, s[0], s[1], wt, wtTop, MAT.innerWall); });\n        // office dividers\n        [-5.5, 0, 5.5].forEach(function (x) { wallX(group, x, -HD, -3, wt, wtTop, MAT.innerWall); });\n        // conference room: north wall z=3 (gap at x=-4), east wall x=-3\n        wallZ(group, 3, -HW, -4.6, wt, wtTop, MAT.innerWall);\n        wallZ(group, 3, -3.4, -3, wt, wtTop, MAT.innerWall);\n        wallX(group, -3, 3, HD, wt, wtTop, MAT.innerWall);\n        // lounge: north wall z=3 (gap at x=4), west wall x=3\n        wallZ(group, 3, 3, 3.4, wt, wtTop, MAT.innerWall);\n        wallZ(group, 3, 4.6, HW, wt, wtTop, MAT.innerWall);\n        wallX(group, 3, 3, HD, wt, wtTop, MAT.innerWall);\n\n        // ---- offices: desk + chair + nodes ----\n        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {\n            const cx = placeChairAt(group, makeOfficeChair(), cx, y, -6.55, Math.PI);\n\n            const doorWp = \"office\" + L + \"_door\";\n            const deskWp = \"office\" + L + \"_desk\";\n            addNode(nodes, doorWp, cx, y, -2.5);\n            addNode(nodes, deskWp, cx, y, -6.55);\n            link(nodes, doorWp, deskWp);\n            link(nodes, doorWp, cx < 0 ? \"hallNW\" : \"hallNE\");\n            // the person sits facing the monitor (-Z) with the chair's\n            // backrest behind them (+Z side)\n            sitTargets[deskWp] = { sit: true, facing: Math.PI };\n            desks.push({\n                id: \"f\" + floorNumber + \"_\" + L,\n                floor: floorNumber, letter: L,\n                deskWp: deskWp, doorWp: doorWp\n            });\n        }\n\n        // ---- conference room ----\n        const confTable = makeConferenceTable();\n        confTable.position.set(-7, y, 6.0);\n        group.add(confTable);\n        const confSeatDefs = [\n            { x: -7.75, z: 4.95, ry: 0 }, { x: -6.25, z: 4.95, ry: 0 },\n            { x: -7.75, z: 7.05, ry: Math.PI }, { x: -6.25, z: 7.05, ry: Math.PI }\n        ];\n        addNode(nodes, \"conf_door\", -4, 4.6);\n        addNode(nodes, \"water_cooler\", 9.5, y, 3.7);\n        link(nodes, \"lounge_door\", \"hallSE\");\n        link(nodes, \"lounge_door\", \"lounge_center\");\n        link(nodes,   // bistro tables (2x2 grid), chairs east+west of each\n        const bistroPos = [[-8.8, 2.6], [-5.4, 2.6], [-8.8, 6.0], [-5.4, 6.0]];\n        const bistroChairs = [];\n            // car speed, world units per second\n\n    // Logical interior spots (car-local coordinates; car floor at y=0).\n    const SPOT_POSITIONS = [\n        { x: -0.7, y: 0, z: -0.7 },\n        { x: 0.7, y: 0, z: -0.7 },\n        { x: -0.7, y: 0, z: 0.45 },\n        { x: 0.7, y: 0, z: 0.45 }\n    ];\n\n    class ElevatorLogic {\n        constructor(opts) {\n            opts = opts || {};\n            this.floorCount = opts.floorCount !== undefined ? opts.floorCount : 6;\n            this.maxCapacity = opts.maxCapacity !== undefined ? opts.maxCapacity : 4;\n            this.floorHeight = opts.floorHeight !== undefined ? opts.floorHeight : 3.4;\n\n            this.DOOR_ANIM_S = DOOR_ANIM_S;\n            this.MIN_DOOR_OPEN_S = MIN_DOOR_OPEN_S;\n            this.MAX_DOOR_OPEN_S = MAX_DOOR_OPEN_S;\n            this.SPEED = SPEED;\n\n            this.reset();\n        }\n\n        reset() {\n            this.state = STATES.IDLE;\n            this.y = 0;                    // car floor world height\n            this.currentFloor = 0;\n            this.targetFloor = null;\n            this.direction = 0;            // +1 up, -1 down, 0 idle\n            this.upCalls = new Set();\n            this.downCalls = new Set();\n            this.destinations = new Set();\n            this.passengers = new Set();\n            this.pendingBoarders = new Set();\n            this.pendingDisembark = new Set();\n            this.spotOccupancy = new Array(SPOT_POSITIONS.length).fill(null);\n            this.doorProgress = 0;         // 0 closed .. 1 open\n            this.doorOpenTimer = 0;\n            this.doorTimer = 0;\n            this.servedThisDoorCycle = false;\n            this.lastServedFloor = null;   // guard: floor served by the most recent door cycle\n        }\n\n        // ---- public call/destination API -------------------------------\n\n        callUp(floor) {\n            if (floor >= 0 && floor < this.floorCount) this.upCalls.add(floor);\n        }\n\n        callDown(floor) {\n            if (floor >= 0 && floor < this.floorCount) this.downCalls.add(floor);\n        }\n\n        pressDestination(floor) {\n            if (floor >= 0 && floor < this.floorCount && floor !== null) {\n                if (floor !== this.currentFloor || this.state === STATES.MOVING) {\n                    this.destinations.add(floor);\n                } else if (this.state !== STATES.DOOR_OPEN && this.state !== STATES.DOOR_OPENING) {\n                    this.destinations.add(floor);\n                }\n                // pressing your current floor while doors are open is a no-op\n            }\n        }\n\n        isAcceptingAt(floor, direction) {\n            if (this.state !== STATES.DOOR_OPEN) return false;\n            if (this.currentFloor !== floor) return false;\n            if (this.direction === 0 || direction === 0) return true;\n            if (this.direction === direction) return true;\n            // accept opposite-direction boarders only when no more work ahead\n            return !this._hasWorkInDirection(this.direction, floor);\n        }\n\n        currentCapacityFree() {\n            return this.maxCapacity - (this.passengers.size + this.pendingBoarders.size);\n        }\n\n        reserveBoardingSpot(person) {\n            if (this.currentCapacityFree() <= 0) return null;\n            for (let i = 0; i < this.spotOccupancy.length; i++) {\n                if (this.spotOccupancy[i] === null) {\n                    this.spotOccupancy[i] = person;\n                    this.pendingBoarders.add(person);\n                    const p = SPOT_POSITIONS[i];\n                    return { index: i, x: p.x, y: p.y, z: p.z };\n                }\n            }\n            return null;\n        }\n\n        completeBoard(person) {\n            if (this.pendingBoarders.has(person)) {\n                this.pendingBoarders.delete(person);\n                this.passengers.add(person);\n            }\n        }\n\n        cancelBoard(person) {\n            // boarder gave up (car left, etc.) — free the spot\n            this.pendingBoarders.delete(person);\n            for (let i = 0; i < this.spotOccupancy.length; i++) {\n                if (this.spotOccupancy[i] === person) this.spotOccupancy[i] = null;\n            }\n        }\n\n        registerDisembark(person) {\n            if (this.passengers.has(person)) {\n                this.pendingDisembark.add(person);\n            }\n        }\n\n        completeDisembark(person) {\n            this.pendingDisembark.delete(person);\n            this.passengers.delete(person);\n            for (let i = 0; i < this.spotOccupancy.length; i++) {\n                if (this.spotOccupancy[i] === person) this.spotOccupancy[i] = null;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // ---- internal helpers ------------------------------------------\n\n        _hasWorkInDirection(dir, fromFloor) {\n            if (dir > 0) {\n                for (let f = fromFloor + 1; f < this.floorCount; f++) {\n                    if (this.destinations.has(f) || this.upCalls.has(f) || this.downCalls.has(f)) return true;\n                }\n            } else if (dir < 0) {\n                for (let f = fromFloor - 1; f >= 0; f--) {\n                    if (this.destinations.has(f) || this.upCalls.has(f) || this.downCalls.has(f)) return true;\n                }\n            }\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        _nearestStopInDirection(dir, fromFloor) {\n            // Nearest destination or matching-direction call strictly ahead.\n            // A non-matching call at the far end is also pickable (turnaround).\n            if (dir === 0) return null;\n            let nearestServe = null;   // destination or matching call\n            let farthestCall = null;   // any call ahead (turnaround target)\n            if (dir > 0) {\n                for (let f = fromFloor + 1; f < this.floorCount; f++) {\n                    if (nearestServe === null && (this.destinations.has(f) || this.upCalls.has(f))) nearestServe = f;\n                    if (this.destinations.has(f) || this.upCalls.has(f) || this.downCalls.has(f)) farthestCall = f;\n                }\n            } else {\n                for (let f = fromFloor - 1; f >= 0; f--) {\n                    if (nearestServe === null && (this.destinations.has(f) || this.downCalls.has(f))) nearestServe = f;\n                    if (this.destinations.has(f) || this.upCalls.has(f) || this.downCalls.has(f)) farthestCall = f;\n                }\n            }\n            if (nearestServe !== null) return nearestServe;\n            return farthestCall; // ride to the far call and reverse there\n        }\n\n        _nearestAnyStop(fromFloor, excludeFloor) {\n            let best = null, bestDist = Infinity;\n            for (let f = 0; f < this.floorCount; f++) {\n                if (f === excludeFloor) continue;\n                if (this.destinations.has(f) || this.upCalls.has(f) || this.downCalls.has(f)) {\n                    const d = Math.abs(f - fromFloor);\n                    if (d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; best = f; }\n                }\n            }\n            return best;\n        }\n\n        _pickNextTarget() {\n            // Called at door-close (and from IDLE). Decide the next target floor.\n            const here = this.currentFloor;\n            const hasRiders = this.passengers.size > 0 || this.pendingBoarders.size > 0;\n\n            // Passenger destinations outrank everything else, especially\n            // same-floor hall calls (no full-car lobby starvation).\n            if (hasRiders && this.destinations.size > 0) {\n                let target = null;\n                if (this.direction !== 0) {\n                    target = this._nearestDestInDirection(this.direction, here);\n                    if (target === null) target = this._nearestDestInDirection(-this.direction, here);\n                }\n                if (target === null) {\n                    let bestDist = Infinity;\n                    this.destinations.forEach((f) => {\n                        const d = Math.abs(f - here);\n                        if (f !== here && d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; target = f; }\n                    });\n                }\n                if (target !== null) return target;\n            }\n\n            // Continue in current direction if there's work ahead.\n            if (this.direction !== 0) {\n                const ahead = this._nearestStopInDirection(this.direction, here);\n                if (ahead !== null) return ahead;\n                const behind = this._nearestStopInDirection(-this.direction, here);\n                if (behind !== null) return behind;\n            }\n\n            // Same-floor hall call while idle/empty. Skipped right after a\n            // door cycle at this floor (servedThisDoorCycle) so leftover\n            // re-pressed calls can't force an immediate reopen; once the car\n            // is IDLE that flag clears and a fresh call reopens normally.\n            if (!hasRiders && !this.servedThisDoorCycle &&\n                (this.upCalls.has(here) || this.downCalls.has(here))) {\n                return here;\n            }\n\n            // Nearest active call/destination anywhere else.\n            const exclude = this.servedThisDoorCycle ? here : -1;\n            const any = this._nearestAnyStop(here, exclude);\n            if (any !== null) return any;\n            return null;\n        }\n\n        _nearestDestInDirection(dir, fromFloor) {\n            if (dir > 0) {\n                for (let f = fromFloor + 1; f < this.floorCount; f++) {\n                    if (this.destinations.has(f)) return f;\n                }\n            } else if (dir < 0) {\n                for (let f = fromFloor - 1; f >= 0; f--) {\n                    if (this.destinations.has(f)) return f;\n                }\n            }\n            return null;\n        }\n\n        _arriveServe() {\n            // Door-open arrival housekeeping: clear served stops.\n            const here = this.currentFloor;\n            this.destinations.delete(here);\n            if (this.direction > 0) {\n                this.upCalls.delete(here);\n                if (!this._hasWorkInDirection(1, here)) {\n                    // no more work ahead — also serve the down call here, and reverse intent\n                    this.downCalls.delete(here);\n                    if (this._hasWorkInDirection(-1, here)) this.direction = -1;\n                    else this.direction = 0;\n                }\n            } else if (this.direction < 0) {\n                this.downCalls.delete(here);\n                if (!this._hasWorkInDirection(-1, here)) {\n                    this.upCalls.delete(here);\n                    if (this._hasWorkInDirection(1, here)) this.direction = 1;\n                    else this.direction = 0;\n                }\n            } else {\n                // idle arrival: infer direction from remaining work, clear both\n                if (this.upCalls.has(here) && this.downCalls.has(here)) {\n                    const up = this._hasWorkInDirection(1, here);\n                    const down = this._hasWorkInDirection(-1, here);\n                    this.upCalls.delete(here);\n                    this.downCalls.delete(here);\n                    this.direction = up ? 1 : (down ? -1 : 0);\n                } else if (this.upCalls.has(here)) {\n                    this.upCalls.delete(here);\n                    this.direction = 1;\n                } else if (this.downCalls.has(here)) {\n                    this.downCalls.delete(here);\n                    this.direction = -1;\n                }\n            }\n            this.servedThisDoorCycle = true;\n            this.lastServedFloor = here;\n        }\n\n        // ---- main tick --------------------------------------------------\n\n        tick(dt) {\n            if (dt <= 0) return;\n            // Cap a single physics step; long frames advance in sub-steps so\n            // a high timeScale doesn't tunnel the car through floors.\n            let remaining = dt;\n            const MAX_STEP = 0.25;\n            while (remaining > 0) {\n                const step = Math.min(MAX_STEP, remaining);\n                const wasOpen = this.state === STATES.DOOR_OPEN;\n                this._step(step);\n                remaining -= step;\n                // Yield the moment the doors reach DOOR_OPEN: at high time\n                // scales a whole frame can exceed the min-open window, and\n                // riders/waiters need one observable DOOR_OPEN frame to\n                // register boarding/disembarking before the timer runs.\n                if (!wasOpen && this.state === STATES.DOOR_OPEN) break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        _step(dt) {\n            switch (this.state) {\n                case STATES.IDLE: {\n                    const target = this._pickNextTarget();\n                    if (target === null) break;\n                    if (target === this.currentFloor) {\n                        // serve a hall call at this floor\n                        if (this.direction === 0) {\n                            if (this.upCalls.has(target) && !this.downCalls.has(target)) this.direction = 1;\n                            else if (this.downCalls.has(target) && !this.upCalls.has(target)) this.direction = -1;\n                        }\n                        this.targetFloor = target;\n                        this.state = STATES.DOOR_OPENING;\n                        this.doorTimer = 0;\n                    } else {\n                        this.targetFloor = target;\n                        this.direction = target > this.currentFloor ? 1 : -1;\n                        this.state = STATES.MOVING;\n                        this.servedThisDoorCycle = false;\n                    }\n                    break;\n                }\n\n                case STATES.MOVING: {\n                    // Re-evaluate: is there a closer stop in the same direction?\n                    if (this.direction > 0) {\n                        const next = Math.ceil((this.y + 1e-6) / this.floorHeight);\n                        for (let f = next; f < this.targetFloor; f++) {\n                            if (this.destinations.has(f) ||\n                                (this.upCalls.has(f) && this.currentCapacityFree() > 0)) {\n                                // only shorten if we can still decelerate \"in time\"\n                                if (f * this.floorHeight >= this.y - 1e-6) {\n                                    this.targetFloor = f;\n                                    break;\n                                }\n                            }\n                        }\n                    } else if (this.direction < 0) {\n                        const next = Math.floor((this.y - 1e-6) / this.floorHeight);\n                        for (let f = next; f > this.targetFloor; f--) {\n                            if (this.destinations.has(f) ||\n                                (this.downCalls.has(f) && this.currentCapacityFree() > 0)) {\n                                if (f * this.floorHeight <= this.y + 1e-6) {\n                                    this.targetFloor = f;\n                                    break;\n                                }\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n\n                    const targetY = this.targetFloor * this.floorHeight;\n                    const dy = targetY - this.y;\n                    const move = SPEED * dt;\n                    if (Math.abs(dy) <= move) {\n                        this.y = targetY;\n                        this.currentFloor = this.targetFloor;\n                        this.state = STATES.DOOR_OPENING;\n                        this.doorTimer = 0;\n                    } else {\n                        this.y += Math.sign(dy) * move;\n                        // keep currentFloor as the nearest floor for indicators\n                        this.currentFloor = Math.round(this.y / this.floorHeight);\n                    }\n                    break;\n                }\n\n                case STATES.DOOR_OPENING: {\n                    this.doorTimer += dt;\n                    this.doorProgress = Math.min(1, this.doorTimer / DOOR_ANIM_S);\n                    if (this.doorProgress >= 1) {\n                        this.state = STATES.DOOR_OPEN;\n                        this.doorOpenTimer = 0;\n                        this._arriveServe();\n                    }\n                    break;\n                }\n\n                case STATES.DOOR_OPEN: {\n                    this.doorOpenTimer += dt;\n                    const held = this.pendingBoarders.size > 0 || this.pendingDisembark.size > 0;\n                    const minElapsed = this.doorOpenTimer >= MIN_DOOR_OPEN_S;\n                    const capFired = this.doorOpenTimer >= MAX_DOOR_OPEN_S;\n                    if ((minElapsed && !held) || capFired) {\n                        if (capFired && held) {\n                            // safety: abandon stuck handshakes so the car can move on\n                            this.pendingDisembark.clear();\n                            this.pendingBoarders.forEach((p) => this.cancelBoard(p));\n                        }\n                        this.state = STATES.DOOR_CLOSING;\n                        this.doorTimer = 0;\n                    }\n                    break;\n                }\n\n                case STATES.DOOR_CLOSING: {\n                    this.doorTimer += dt;\n                    this.doorProgress = Math.max(0, 1 - this.doorTimer / DOOR_ANIM_S);\n                    if (this.doorProgress <= 0) {\n                        const target = this._pickNextTarget();\n                        if (target === null) {\n                            this.state = STATES.IDLE;\n                            this.targetFloor = null;\n                            this.direction = 0;\n                            this.servedThisDoorCycle = false;\n                        } else if (target === this.currentFloor) {\n                            // Guard: never reopen at the floor just served (this.destinations.size > 0 || this.passengers.size > 0)) {\n                                // force pick a destination instead\n                                let alt = null, bestDist = Infinity;\n                                this.destinations.forEach((f) => {\n                                    if (f !== this.currentFloor) {\n                                        const d = Math.abs(f -                    this.state = STATES.MOVING;\n                                    this.servedThisDoorCycle = false;\n                                } else {\n                                    this.state = STATES.IDLE;\n                                    this.targetFloor = null;\n                                    this.direction = 0;\n                                    this.servedThisDoorCycle = false;\n                                }\n                            } else {\n                                this.state = STATES.DOOR_OPENING;\n this.servedThisDoorCycle = false;\n                        }\n                    }\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    ElevatorLogic.STATES = // fixed deterministic tick\nconst results = [];\n\nfunction test(name, fn) {\n    try {\n        fn();\n        results.push({ name, ok: true });\n        console.log(`PASS  ${name}`);\n    } catch (err) {\n        results.push({ name, ok: false, err });\n        console.log(`FAIL  ${name}`);\n        console.log(`      ${err.message}`);\n    }\n}\n\n// --- helpers ---------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction tickUntil(ev, pred, maxTicks, label) {\n    maxTicks = maxTicks || 5000;\n    for (let i = 0; i < maxTicks; i++) {\n        if (pred(ev)) return i;\n        ev.tick(DT);\n    }\n    throw new Error(`tickUntil(${label || \"predicate\"}) exceeded ${maxTicks} ticks ` +\n        `(state=${ev.state} floor=${ev.currentFloor} target=${ev.targetFloor})`);\n}\n\nfunction runUntilDoorOpenAt(ev, floor, maxTicks) {\n    return tickUntil(ev,\n        (e) => e.state === \"DOOR_OPEN\" && e.currentFloor === floor,\n        maxTicks, `DOOR_OPEN@${floor}`);\n}\n\nfunction runUntilDoorClosed(ev, maxTicks) {\n    // run until doors finish closing (state leaves DOOR_OPEN/DOOR_CLOSING)\n    tickUntil(ev, (e) => e.state === \"DOOR_OPEN\", maxTicks, \"reach DOOR_OPEN\");\n    return tickUntil(ev,\n        (e) => e.state === \"IDLE\" || e.state === \"MOVING\",\n        maxTicks, \"doors closed\");\n}\n\nfunction boardRider(ev, name, destFloor) {\n    const spot = ev.reserveBoardingSpot(name);\n    assert.ok(spot, `${name} should get a boarding spot`);\n    ev.completeBoard(name);\n    if (destFloor !== undefined && destFloor !== null) ev.pressDestination(destFloor);\n    return spot;\n}\n\n// --- 1. Lobby rush with more callers than capacity ---------------------------\n\ntest(\"lobby rush: full car leaves floor 0 despite re-pressed UP calls\", () => {\n    const ev = new ElevatorLogic({ floorCount: 6, maxCapacity: 4 });\n    ev.callUp(0);\n    runUntilDoorOpenAt(ev, 0);\n\n    // four riders board, press upper floors\n    boardRider(ev, \"p1\", 2);\n    boardRider(ev, \"p2\", 3);\n    boardRider(ev, \"p3\", 5);\n    boardRider(ev, \"p4\", 3);\n    assert.strictEqual(ev.currentCapacityFree(), 0, \"car should be full\");\n    assert.strictEqual(ev.reserveBoardingSpot(\"p5\"), null, \"5th rider must be rejected\");\n\n    // leftover lobby waiters keep pressing UP every tick\n    let sawReopenAtZero = false;\n    let leftFloorZero = false;\n    for (let i = 0; i < 600; i++) {\n        ev.callUp(0); // re-press like real waiters\n        const prevState = ev.state;\n        ev.tick(DT);\n        if (prevState === \"DOOR_CLOSING\" && ev.state === \"DOOR_OPENING\" && ev.currentFloor === 0) {\n            sawReopenAtZero = true;\n        }\n        if (ev.state === \"MOVING\" && ev.targetFloor > 0) { leftFloorZero = true; break; }\n    }\n    assert.ok(!sawReopenAtZero, \"doors must not reopen at floor 0 while destinations exist\");\n    assert.ok(leftFloorZero, \"car must depart upward with its passengers\");\n    assert.ok(ev.targetFloor > 0, `next target must be above floor 0, got ${ev.targetFloor}`);\n    assert.ok(ev.upCalls.has(0), \"leftover UP call stays queued for the next trip\");\n});\n\n// --- 2. Repeated hall-call pressing cannot starve riders ---------------------\n\ntest(\"repeated callUp(0) cannot starve riders' destinations\", () => {\n    const ev = new ElevatorLogic({ floorCount: 6, maxCapacity: 4 });\n    ev.callUp(0);\n    runUntilDoorOpenAt(ev, 0);\n    boardRider(ev, \"a\", 3);\n    boardRider(ev, \"b\", 5);\n\n    let reachedDest = false;\n    for (let i = 0; i < 3000; i++) {\n        ev.callUp(0); // hammer the lobby button every tick\n        ev.tick(DT);\n        if (ev.state === \"DOOR_OPEN\" && (ev.currentFloor === 3 || ev.currentFloor === 5)) {\n            reachedDest = true;\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    assert.ok(reachedDest, \"car must still reach a passenger destination\");\n});\n\n// --- 4. }\n\n    function moveToward(agent, target, dt, speed) {\n        // returns remaining distance after the move; rotates to face motion\n        const g = agent.group;\n        const dx = target.x - return dist - step;\n    }\n\n    function startAction(agent, act) {\n        const g = agent.group;\n        switch (act.type) {\n            case \"WALK_TO_WP\": {\n                const nodes =      // (which also runs frames) abandons the boarder mid-walk.\n                const floor = agent.floorIdx;\n\n                if (act._phase === 0) {            // reserve a spot\n
